公司簡介
CORPORATE PROFILE


華潤水泥控股有限公司於二零零三年三月十三日根據開曼群島公司法在開曼群島註冊成立為一
家獲豁免的有限公司，並為華潤集團所有水泥及混凝土業務的控股公司。

本公司曾於二零零三年七月二十九日以介紹形式在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市。於二零零
六年，本公司被華潤（集團）有限公司私有化成為順創投資有限公司（現稱華潤集團（水泥）有限公
司）的全資附屬公司，並於二零零六年七月二十六日撤銷其股份在聯交所的上市地位。

本公司於二零零九年十月六日以全球發售形式重新於聯交所主板上市。於本報告日期，本公司已
發行股份總數為6,982,937,817股，其中華潤集團持有約68.72%的本公司已發行股份。

China Resources Cement Holdings Limited was incorporated on 13 March 2003 in the Cayman Islands as an
exempted company with limited liability under The Companies Law of the Cayman Islands and is the holding
company of all the cement and concrete operations of the China Resources Group.


The Company was listed on the main board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited on 29 July 2003 by way
of introduction. In 2006, the Company was privatized as a wholly-owned subsidiary of Smooth Concept Investments
Limited (currently known as CRH (Cement) Limited) by China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited and the listing
of the Company’s shares on the Stock Exchange was withdrawn on 26 July 2006.


The Company was re-listed on the main board of the Stock Exchange on 6 October 2009 by way of a global offering.
As at the date of this report, the total number of issued shares of the Company was 6,982,937,817 shares, of which
China Resources Group is holding approximately 68.72% of the issued shares of the Company.

華潤水泥控股有限公司為中國華南地區最大及最                           China Resources Cement Holdings Limited is the largest and the most

流網絡，我們的產品主要在廣東、廣西、福建、海                          mainly sold in Guangdong, Guangxi, Fujian, Hainan, Yunnan, Guizhou

修建基建工程如鐵路、公路、地鐵、橋樑、機場、                          logistics networks. Our products are primarily used in the construction
港口、水壩、水電站及核電站等，並用於建造高層                          of infrastructure projects such as railways, highways, subways, bridges,
建築物以及市郊與鄉鎮發展。                                   airports, ports, dams, hydroelectric power stations and nuclear
                                                power stations, as well as the construction of high-rise buildings and

均配備餘熱發電設備以降低能耗，氮氧化物、顆粒         of our clinker production lines are equipped with residual heat recovery
物及二氧化硫排放濃度均符合國家標準。此外，我         generators to reduce energy consumption. The emission levels of nitrogen

為保持有效投資力度，促進內需和結構調整，國務                             In order to maintain effective investment efforts and foster domestic
院辦公廳於二零一八年十月發佈《關於保持基礎                              demand and structural adjustment, in October 2018, the General Office
設施領域補短板力度的指導意見》，提出加快推                              of the State Council issued the “Guiding Opinions on Maintaining Efforts
進項目前期工作和開工建設、保障在建項目順利                              to Bolster Areas of Weakness in Infrastructure”, which proposed policies
實施等政策措施，重點投向鐵路、公路及水運、機                             and measures such as accelerating the launch of preliminary work
場、農村農業、生態環保等九大領域。隨著這些政                             and the commencement of construction of projects and securing the
策措施的貫徹落實，全國基礎設施投資整體呈現                              smooth implementation of projects under construction with a focus on
平穩增長，為水泥需求奠定基礎。                                    nine major scopes including railways, highways and waterways, airports,
                                                   agricultural industry in rural areas and ecological environmental protection.
                                                   Following the implementation of these policies and measures, the national
                                                   infrastructure investment showed an overall steady growth, which had laid
                                                   the foundation for cement demand.

在房地產調控政策下，全國房地產市場大致穩定，                             On the back of regulation and control policies on property, the housing

至人民幣15.0萬億元。全國房地產投資按年增長                            commodity housing sold in China increased by 1.3% over last year to
9.5%至人民幣12.0萬億元。其中，房屋新開工面積                         1,720 million m2 and the sales amount increased by 12.2% to RMB15.0

理，實現環境友好型企業的轉型。同時，本集團致   urban sludge and hazardous industrial waste by use of cement kilns

主要風險及不確定性                PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES

水泥及混凝土是基礎設施、房地產建設及農村發    Cement and concrete are basic building materials for infrastructure
展的基本建築材料。水泥消費量與經濟發展及固    projects, property construction and rural development. Cement
定資產投資高度相關。由於水泥及混凝土是沉重    consumption is highly correlated to economic development and FAI. As
的建築材料，在區域市場競爭環境下，生產商在    cement and concrete are heavy building materials, the key to success of a
合理運輸距離內的核心競爭優勢是業務成功的關    producer’s business is highly dependent on its core competitive advantage
鍵。本集團業務運營區域內經營環境及其所影響    within the reasonable transportation radius in a competitive environment of
的水泥及混凝土產品銷售價格，是本集團經營表    the regional market. The performance of the Group’s business operations
現的主要影響因素。此外，煤炭成本是水泥生產中   is therefore subject to certain major factors such as the business
最重要，以及導致成本波動的主要原因。因此，煤   environment in the regions where the Group has business operation,
價大幅波動是影響本集團及水泥行業表現的其他    which significantly affects the selling prices of the cement and concrete
主要潛在風險及不明朗因素。            products. Besides, coal cost represents the most significant and volatile
                         component of the cost of cement production. Accordingly, the substantial
                         fluctuation of coal price poses the other principal risk and uncertainty
                         affecting the performance of the Group and the cement industry at large.

潛在出售事項                                            POTENTIAL DISPOSAL

本公司於二零一八年十一月五日及二零一八年                              As announced by the Company on 5 November 2018 and 27 December
十二月二十七日發出公告，本公司的全資附屬公司                            2018, China Resources Cement Investments Limited, the Company’s

此外，本集團正探索業務運營區域內的骨料發展           In addition, the Group is exploring opportunities to develop the aggregate
機會，未來將充分發揮水泥、混凝土、骨料與裝配          business in the regions where the Group has business operations. In
式建築業務的協同優勢，實現產業一體化發展，           the future, the Group will fully utilize the synergetic advantages in its

 本集團嚴格執行《華潤水泥管理手冊》     We strictly implement the “EHS Management Series” of
「EHS管理篇」及監控環境管理體系，下發   the “Management Manual” of the Company and monitor
 有關協同處置項目污染物排放監測管理的    the environmental management system by issuing definite
 明確要求。本集團的星級管理評價體系基    requirements on the monitor and management of pollutant
 於行業對標或內部對標結果設定目標值，    emissions relating to co-processing projects. The Group’s star-
 對各水泥生產基地在環境、健康和安全等    grading management appraisal system sets targets based on
 方面進行全面評價，從而提升管理水平，    the results of industry benchmarking or internal benchmarking.
 推動企業的可持續發展。           Comprehensive appraisal on the environment, health and safety
                       aspects of every cement production plant is conducted, which
                       enhances the management standard and fosters corporate
                       sustainable development.


本集團的主要產品為水泥、熟料及混凝      During the production and manufacturing process of our major
土，生產製造過程中主要產生的排放物為     products (namely, cement, clinker and concrete), the Group
粉塵、氮氧化物及二氧化硫等。本集團的     mainly emits pollutants including dust, Nitrogen Oxides and
熟料生產線在物料處理、輸送過程中均採     sulphur dioxide. Fully enclosed delivery is adopted during
用全封閉式運送，物料裝卸過程均在封閉     materials treatment and delivery process at all the clinker

      產運營及長期發展規劃。                                         as well as green, ecologically and environmentally friendly mining

                                                          operation and long-term development plans of the Company.

      對於開採區域，本集團通過礦內水資源循                                  construction are avoided to the greatest possible extent. For
      環利用，污水零排放，廢油及時回收利用，                                 the mining areas, through reutilization of water resources in the
      減震、弱震爆破技術的應用等措施，減少                                  mines, there is zero discharge of wastewater. Measures for timely
      生產運營活動對生物生存環境的影響，進                                  recycling of waste oil and application of shock reduction and
      而有效保證區域內物種的棲存和延續。                                   weak shock blasting technologies are taken, in order to reduce
      在礦山開採結束後進行覆土，種上藤類植                                  the impact of production and operation activities on ecological

1.    僱傭                                           1.     EMPLOYMENT
      一般資料                                                General Information

      為使僱員分類更能反映各僱員的工作職                                   In order to better reflect the work functions of each staff by
      能，自二零一八年度起，本集團將原來安                                  classification, since 2018, the Group had re-classified the roles
      全、環保及研發相關僱員的職能由過去披                                  relevant to the functions of safety, environmental protection and
      露為「財務及行政」納入「技術人員」、

II、營運慣例                         II、OPERATING PRACTICES

1.   供應鏈管理                      1.   SUPPLY CHAIN MANAGEMENT

     本集團持續完善供應商管理措施，不斷優              The Group persistently improves its supplier management
     化自主開發的供應商關係管理系統，從供              measures and continuously optimizes its self-developed supplier
     應商准入、合作、評價、績效反饋等方面              relationship management system, in order to manage and provide
     對8,007名供應商（二零一七年：6,631名）        guidance to 8,007 suppliers (6,631 in 2017) regarding their
     進行管理與輔導，共同建立透明的、可追              admission, cooperation, evaluation and performance feedback
     溯的、穩定長期和可持續發展的供應鏈。              for the joint establishment of a transparent, traceable, stable,
     根據公司戰略、採購物資特點等制定供應              long-term and sustainable supply chain. Corporate strategies
     商開發與選擇標準，多渠道引進優秀供應              and characteristics of procurement materials are taken into
     商，優秀供應商在符合目標成本的前提下              account when we set the criteria for development and selection of
     優先採購；對待整改供應商及時回饋待整              suppliers. Outstanding suppliers are introduced through multiple
     改問題，給予相應協助，限期核查整改結              channels. We would procure from the outstanding suppliers
     果；將不合格供應商調離合格供應商庫。              on a priority basis provided that their prices are consistent with
                                     our target costs. Suppliers which require rectifications would be
                                     provided with our timely feedback on their weaknesses, offered
                                     relevant assistance and examined on their rectification results
                                     upon the deadline. The substandard suppliers would be removed
                                     from our eligible supplier database.


     本集團對供應商或合作夥伴提出的責任               The Group requires our suppliers or business partners to fulfil
     要求包括遵守法規、保護環境、誠信經營              certain responsibilities, including compliance with laws and
     和質量保證等，採購合同均增加附則《供              regulations, environmental protection, integrity operations and
     應商誠信條款》，要求所有供應商簽署並              quality assurance. “Integrity Terms of Suppliers” are incorporated
     遵守相關規定。此外，本集團高度重視供              in all our procurement contracts, which all suppliers are required
     應商安全環保管理，將相關要求納入供應              to enter into and comply with. In addition, the Group pays keen
     商資格審核及合作過程監管中，所有合作              attention to management of suppliers on safety and environmental
     方均須簽署《安全環保協議》；同時，本集             protection by incorporating relevant requirements into our review
     團將「陽光採購」作為採購踐行的首要原              on suppliers’ qualification and supervision during the cooperation
     則，所有採購人員及供應商均須簽署《陽              process. All business partners are required to sign the “Safety

      根據《中華人民共和國刑法》、本集團的                                According to the Criminal Law of the People’s Republic of China,
      員工管理辦法及規章制度，任何人不得利                                the Group’s staff management measures and policies, no person
      用職權索賄、受賄，以不正當手段謀取私                                is allowed to take advantage of his position to demand and receive
      利，不得進行勒索、欺詐及洗黑錢活動。                                bribery or obtain benefits by improper means. It is also not allowed
      一經發現，將其除名永不錄用，案情重大                                to blackmail, conduct fraud or money laundering. Once identified,
      者，將送公安局或司法機關處理。                                   the staff will be dismissed and blacklisted. Significant cases will be
                                                        reported to the police or judicial authority.

                    窰要求的固體廢物投入                                              recyclable and mass-reducing” manner by
                    水泥窰焚燒，在進行水                                              inputting the solid wastes into cement kilns for
                    泥熟料生產的同時實現                                              combustion, taking advantage of the features

                    資源化、減量化處置過                                              alkalinity and large capacity during production of

 B7. 反貪污                   一般披露                                                                            B(II)3    72
 B7. Anti-corruption       General Disclosure
                           有關防止賄賂、勒索、欺詐及洗黑錢的：
                           (a)      政策；及
                           (b)      遵守對發行人有重大影響的相關法律及規例的資料。
                           Information on:
                           (a)      the policies; and
                           (b)      compliance with relevant laws and

獲准許的彌償條文                                 PERMITTED INDEMNITY PROVISION
本公司於年內已購買及維持董事及高級管理人員                    The Company has purchased and maintained directors and officers
責任保險，為本集團之董事及高級管理人員可能                    liability insurance throughout the year, which provides appropriate cover
因其履行職責而招致的潛在第三者法律責任提供                    for the directors and officers of the Group against their potential legal
適當保障，並每年檢討保障範圍程度一次。                      liabilities to third parties that may be incurred in the course of performing
                                         their duties. The level of the coverage is reviewed annually.

      二零年十二月三十一日止為期三年。該協                                    supply to the Group), for the term of three years from 1 January
      議將有助本集團以公平、合理及有競爭力                                    2018 to 31 December 2020. The agreement would enable
      並與一般電網電費較低的價格獲得電力供                                    the Group to secure electricity supply at a fair, reasonable and
      應，因而有助降低本集團的運營成本。根                                    competitive price which is lower than the normal on-grid tariffs,
      據該協議，本集團應付的電費將根據國家                                    which in turn reduces operating costs of the Group. Under the
      及省份供電系統改革政策，並參考公開巿                                    agreement, the electricity fees payable by the Group would be

長期獎勵計劃                     LONG TERM AWARD SCHEME

本公司於二零零九年九月二日採納一項股份獎勵      The Company adopted a share award scheme on 2 September 2009.
計劃。根據上市規則第十七章，股份獎勵計劃並不     The share award scheme does not constitute a share option scheme
構成一項股份期權計劃且為本公司的一項酌情計      pursuant to Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules and is a discretionary scheme
劃。股份獎勵計劃旨在確認本集團管理層及僱員      of the Company. The purpose of the share award scheme is to recognize
（不包括任何董事）過去為本集團的發展作出的貢     the contributions of officers and employees of our Group, excluding any
獻，或為鼓勵經甄選承授人為本集團實現超越目      Director, towards the development of our Group in the past or as incentives
標的盈利及將經甄選承授人的利益與本集團的持      to selected grantees to achieve higher than target profits for our Group and
續增長及發展掛鈎。股份獎勵計劃的有效期為十      to align the interests of the selected grantees with sustainable growth and
年，直至二零一九年九月二日屆滿，除非董事局      development of our Group. The share award scheme is effective for a term
於較早日期酌情終止。                 of ten years until 2 September 2019 unless terminated at the discretion of
                           the Board at an earlier date.

企業管治常規                                         CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES

本公司與董事局致力達致及保持最高標準的企業                          The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and maintaining
管治，並於構建本集團的企業管治守則時採納了                          the highest standards of corporate governance and have adopted the
企業管治守則的原則。董事局認為有效的企業管                          principles of the CG Code in the construction of our corporate governance
治常規為提升股東價值及保障本集團股東及其他                          code. The Board believes that effective corporate governance practices are
持份者權益的基礎。因此，本公司已採納合理的                          fundamental to enhancing shareholder value and safeguarding interests
企業管治原則，以突顯董事局質素、有效的風險                          of our shareholders and other stakeholders. Accordingly, the Company
管理及內部控制、嚴格的披露常規及對所有持份                          has adopted sound corporate governance principles that emphasize on
者的透明度及問責性。                                     the quality of the Board, effective risk management and internal control,
                                               stringent disclosure practices, and transparency and accountability to all
                                               stakeholders.


截至二零一八年十二月三十一日止年度內，本公                          During the year ended 31 December 2018, the Company had complied
司已遵守企業管治守則適用守則條文，惟下文所                          with the applicable code provisions of the CG Code, except for the
披露的偏離事項除外。                                     deviation as disclosed below.

公司組織章程細則，全體董事須由本公司股東於                          subject to re-election by shareholders of the Company at annual general
股東週年大會上重選且至少約每三年輪選一次，                          meetings and at least about once every three years on a rotation basis
故有充足措施確保本公司符合本條文所規定的同                          in accordance with the Articles of Association of the Company, there are
一水平。                                           sufficient measures to ensure the Company complies with the same level

董事局                     THE BOARD

組成                      Composition


董事局負責指引本公司的策略性目標及監管業務   The Board is responsible for directing the strategic objectives and
的管理。董事負責促進本公司業務順利進展及客   overseeing the management of the business of the Company. The
觀地作出符合本公司最佳利益的決策。       Directors are accountable for promoting the success of the Company and
                        making decisions objectively in the best interests of the Company.


為保持高效率運作和經營決策的靈活性與迅捷    In order to maintain highly efficient operation, as well as flexibility and
性，董事局必要時亦將其管理及行政權力轉授予   swiftness in operational decision-making, the Board, when necessary, may
管理層，並就授權行為提供清晰的指示，以避免   delegate its managing and administrative powers to the management,
嚴重妨礙或削弱董事局履行其職權的整體能力。   and provide clear directions regarding such delegation so as to avoid
轉授予董事局轄下各委員會的職責及權力詳載於   seriously impeding or undermining the overall capabilities of the Board in
下文各部份。                  exercising its powers. Duties and authorities delegated to the various board
                        committees are described in the respective sections below.


董事局應根據本公司業務所需而具備適當的技巧   The Board should have a balance of skills and experience appropriate for
和經驗。執行董事及非執行董事的組合應保持平   the requirements of the business of the Company. A balanced composition
衡，以確保董事局的獨立性並可令董事局作出有   of executive Directors and non-executive Directors helps to ensure the
效的獨立判斷。董事局的運作和管理以及本公司   independence of the Board and enables the Board to make independent
業務的日常管理應在董事局的層面清晰劃分，以   judgments effectively. The operation and management of the Board and
確保權力及授權的平衡，而權力不會集中於任何   the day-to-day management of the Company’s business should be clearly
個人。                     divided at the Board level to ensure that there is a balance of power and
                        authority and that power will not be concentrated on any one individual.

獨立性                       Independence


就考慮一名董事是否獨立而言，提名委員會及董     For a Director to be considered independent, the Nomination Committee
事局須信納該董事並無且將不會與本集團有任何     and the Board must be satisfied that the Director does not and will
直接或間接重大關係。於判定董事的獨立性時，     not have any direct or indirect material relationship with the Group. In
提名委員會及董事局依照上市規則所載列的規定     determining the independence of Directors, the Nomination Committee
並視所有獨立非執行董事均為獨立。此外，本公司    and the Board follow the requirements set out in the Listing Rules and
已從所有獨立非執行董事接獲彼等根據上市規則     consider all of the independent non-executive Directors as independent.
第3.13條每年提交的獨立性確認書。每位董事概   In addition, the Company has received annual written confirmation
無與任何其他董事存在或維持任何財務、業務、     of independence from all the independent non-executive Directors in
家族或其他重大╱有關關係。             accordance with Rule 3.13 of the Listing Rules. None of the Directors has
                          or maintained any financial, business, family or other material/relevant
                          relationship with any of the other Directors.

董事局會議                     Board Meetings


董事局預期定期會晤且每年至少四次。於定期的     The Board is expected to meet regularly and at least four times a year.
會議之間，本集團的高級管理層會就本集團業務     Between scheduled meetings, the senior management of the Group
的活動及發展定期向董事提供資料。在董事認為     provides information to Directors on a regular basis regarding the activities
有必要的任何時候，董事可隨時全面獲取本集團     and development in the businesses of the Group. The Directors shall have
的資料及獨立專業意見。               full access to information on the Group and independent professional
                          advice at all times whenever considered necessary by the Directors.


就董事局的定期會議而言，董事將在會議前至少     With respect to regular meetings of the Board, the Directors will be
十四天獲得書面會議通知及在會議前不少於三      given written notice of the meeting at least fourteen days in advance
天獲得議程和所需文件。就其他會議而言，在合     and an agenda together with supporting papers no less than three days
理及切實可行的情況下，董事局盡量獲予最早的     prior to the meeting. For other meetings, Directors are given as much
通知。除本公司組織章程細則所容許的該等情況     advance notice as reasonable and practicable in the circumstances.
外，已於任何合約、交易、安排或向董事局提呈以    Except for those circumstances permitted by the Articles of Association
供考慮的任何其他類別建議中擁有重大權益的董     of the Company, a Director who has a material interest in any contract,
事，將須就相關決議案放棄投票且該董事不會被     transaction, arrangement or any other kind of proposal put forward to the
計入審批相關決議案的法定人數內。          Board for consideration shall abstain from voting on the relevant resolution

主席及總裁的角色及職能              ROLES AND FUNCTIONS OF CHAIRMAN AND
                         CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER

主席                       Chairman


主席與總裁的職權乃分開的。主席批准及監管本    The roles of the Chairman are segregated from the Chief Executive Officer.
公司的策略及政策、年度預算及業務規劃，評估    The Chairman approves and monitors the Company’s strategies and
本公司的表現及監督本公司的管理。         policies, annual budgets and business plans, evaluates the performance
                         of the Company, and supervises the management of the Company.


該等責任的劃分亦有助加強其獨立性及問責性。主   Such division of responsibilities also helps to reinforce their independence
席負責領導及監察董事局的職能，以確保其表現    and accountability. The Chairman is responsible for providing leadership
符合本集團的最佳利益，及董事局會議有計劃及    to, and overseeing the functioning of, the Board to ensure that it acts
有效地舉行。主席亦負責為每次董事局會議設定    in the best interests of the Group and that Board meetings are planned
議程，當中已考慮過董事及公司秘書所建議的事    and conducted in an effective manner. The Chairman is also responsible
宜（如適用）
     。在執行董事及公司秘書的支持下，    for setting the agenda for each Board meeting upon considering, where
主席力求確保全體董事適時獲悉於董事局會議上    appropriate, matters proposed by the Directors and the Company
列舉的問題和及時地獲得充分而準確、清晰、完    Secretary. With the support of executive Directors and the Company
備及可靠的資訊。主席亦積極鼓勵董事著手處理    Secretary, the Chairman seeks to ensure that all Directors are properly
董事局的事務及促進董事局的職能。主席每年與    informed of issues arising at Board meetings and provided in a timely
獨立非執行董事舉行不少於一次沒有其他董事出    manner with adequate information which is accurate, clear, complete
席的當面會議。在主席的領導下，董事局已遵守    and reliable. The Chairman also actively encourages the Directors to be
企業管治常規及程序，並已採取適當措施確保與    engaged in the Board’s affairs and contribute to the Board’s functions.
本集團股東及其他持份者有效溝通。         The Chairman holds physical meetings with the independent non-
                         executive Directors without the presence of other Directors not less than
                         once a year. The Board, under the leadership of the Chairman, has
                         complied with the corporate governance practices and procedures and
                         has taken appropriate steps to ensure effective communication with our
                         shareholders and other stakeholders.


當主席在休假期間而主席的正常職能未能執行，    During the period when the Chairman is absent and the normal functions
董事局可挑選另外一名董事擔當替任主席的崗     of the Chairman cannot be carried out, the Board may choose another
位，直至主席回復執行其正常職能或新主席被董    Director to take the role as the acting Chairman until the Chairman
事局選拔及委任，惟受限於適用法律、規則、規例   resumes carrying out his normal duties or a new Chairman has been
以及本公司組織章程細則之遵守。          elected and appointed by the Board, subject to compliance with
                         applicable laws, rules, regulations and the Articles of Association of the
                         Company.


總裁                       Chief Executive Officer


總裁負責管理本集團的業務，協調整體業務運作、   The Chief Executive Officer is responsible for managing the businesses
參與制定及成功實施董事局所批准的本集團政策    of the Group, coordinating overall business operations, attending to the
及策略，以及就本集團的所有日常運作向董事局    formulation and successful implementation of the Group’s policies and
承擔全部責任。                  strategies approved by the Board and assuming full accountability to the
                         Board for all the day-to-day operations of the Group.

獨立核數師                                             Independent Auditor


審核委員會檢討及監察獨立核數師的獨立性以及                             The Audit Committee reviews and monitors the independent auditor’s
審核程序的客觀性及有效性。審核委員會每年接                             independence and objectivity and effectiveness of the audit process. It
獲獨立核數師函件，確認其獨立性及客觀性，並                             receives each year the letter from the independent auditor confirming their
與獨立核數師的代表舉行會議以考慮將由其提供                             independence and objectivity and holds meetings with representatives
的審核範圍以及非審核服務（如有）的範圍及適當                            of the independent auditor to consider the scope of its audit, and the
性。審核委員會隨後就獨立核數師的委任、重新委                            scope and appropriateness of non-audit services, if any, to be provided
任、解聘、酬金及聘用條款向董事局作出建議。                             by it. The Audit Committee then makes recommendations to the Board
                                                  on the appointment, reappointment, removal, remuneration and terms of
                                                  engagement of the independent auditor.

風險管理及內部監控                                         RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                                                  CONTROL

董事局全權負責並高度關注本集團的風險管理及                             The Board has overall responsibility for and is highly concerned with the
內部監控系統以及風險評估與管理。為履行其職                             Group’s risk management and internal control systems, and assessment
責，董事局力求提升本集團旗下各業務單位的風                             and management of risks. In meeting its responsibility, the Board seeks
險意識，並透過制訂政策和程序，包括界定授權                             to increase risk awareness across the Group’s business operations
的基準，藉以建立一個有助識別與管理風險的架                             and has put in place policies and procedures, including parameters of
構。董事局亦審閱及監察風險管理及內部監控系                             authority delegation, which provide a framework for the identification and
統的有效性，以確保所採用的政策及程序足夠及                             management of risks. It also reviews and monitors the effectiveness of the
適當。                                               risk management and internal control systems to ensure that the policies
                                                  and procedures in place are adequate and appropriate.


匯報與審閱工作包括由執行董事與董事局審批的                             Reporting and review activities include review and approval by the
區域管理層提交的詳盡營運與財務報告、預算和                             executive Directors and the Board of detailed operational and financial
計劃，由董事局對照預算案以審核實際業績，由                             reports, budgets and plans provided by the regional management, review
審核委員會審閱本集團的內部審核職能及風險管                             by the Board of actual results against the budgets, review by the Audit
理職能所持續進行的工作，以及由執行董事定期                             Committee of the ongoing work of the Group’s internal audit function and
進行的業務回顧。                                          risk management function, as well as regular business reviews by the
                                                  executive Directors.


儘管上述程序旨在確定與管理可能對本集團在實                             Whilst these procedures are designed to identify and manage risks
現其業務目標時存有不利影響的風險，但並不能                             that could adversely impact the achievement of the Group’s business
保證絕對不會出現重大失實陳述、錯誤、遺漏或                             objectives, they do not provide absolute assurance against material
欺詐。                                               misstatement, errors, omissions or fraud.

的可能性與其潛在的財務影響。執行董事審閱涵    budgets and forecasts, the management identifies, evaluates and reports

股東提名他人參選董事的程序                         Procedures for shareholders to nominate a person for election as a
                                      Director


本公司組織章程細則第16.4條規定，任何未經董               Article 16.4 of the Articles of Association of the Company provides that no
事局推薦的人士均不符合資格於任何股東大會上                 person shall, unless recommended by the Board, be eligible for election
獲選為董事，除非於指定進行該選舉的大會通告                 to the office of Director at any general meeting unless during the period,

收入確認                     Revenue recognition

年內，我們注意到，由於採納新會計準則，有關收   During the year, due to the adoption of a new accounting standard, a
益確認的會計政策發生變動。收益確認的新會計    change in accounting policy on revenue recognition was noted. The new
政策已於綜合財務報表附註3詳述。管理層須評估   accounting policy for revenue recognition has been detailed in Note 3 to
過渡影響對採納新政策的影響並須就評估何時達    the consolidated financial statements. Management is required to assess
成履約義務的時間作出判斷。            the impacts for the transition to account for the impact from adopting the
                         new policy and it needs to make judgment in assessing the timing when
                         the performance obligation has been satisfied.


管理層監察合約條款及釐定銷售貨品收入於實體    Management inspected the contract terms and determined that revenue

商譽減值評估                                             Impairment assessment of goodwill

由於履行該評估時涉及重大判斷及假設，故我們                              We identified the impairment assessment of goodwill as a key audit matter
確定商譽之減值評估作為關鍵審計事項。                                 due to the involvement of significant judgements and assumptions in the
                                                   performance of this assessment.

誠如綜合財務報表附註4所載，釐定商譽是否存在                             As set out in Note 4 to the consolidated financial statements, determining
減值須估計獲分配商譽的現金產生單位（「現金產                             whether goodwill is impaired requires an estimation of the value in use of
生單位」）的使用價值。計算使用價值要求 貴集                             the cash-generating units (“CGUs”) to which goodwill has been allocated.
團估計預期由現金產生單位產生的未來現金流量                              The value in use calculation required the Group to estimate the future
及適當的折現率以計算現值。估計不明朗因素主                              cash flows expected to arise from the CGUs and a suitable discount rate
要包括毛利率、折現率及增長率。                                    in order to calculate the present value. The estimation uncertainty mainly
                                                   included gross margin, discount rate and growth rate.


誠如綜合財務報表附註19所披露，於二零一八年                             The carrying amount of goodwill as at 31 December 2018 was
十二月三十一日，商譽的賬面值為1,193,827,000港                      HK$1,193,827,000 and for the purpose of impairment testing, as
元。就減值測試而言，商譽已分配至由水泥經營分                             disclosed in Note 19 to the consolidated financial statements, goodwill has
部及混凝土經營分部組成的現金產生單位組別及                              been allocated to the groups of CGUs comprising the cement operating
於二零一八年十二月三十一日，分配至彼等的商譽                             segment and concrete operating segment and the carrying amounts of
的賬面值分別為1,145,739,000港元及48,088,000港                 goodwill allocated to them were HK$1,145,739,000 and HK$48,088,000,
元。 貴集團管理層已評估並認為截至二零一八                              respectively, at 31 December 2018. The management of the Group
年十二月三十一日止年度概無重大商譽減值。                               assessed and concluded that there was no significant impairment of
                                                   goodwill for the year ended 31 December 2018.

                         included:

陣以計算預期信貸虧損。矩陣初始基於本集團歷                              consolidated financial statements. Management used a provision matrix

1.   一般資料                                        1.   GENERAL

     本公司於二零零三年三月十三日根據開曼                               The Company was incorporated in the Cayman Islands as an
     群島公司法（二零零二年修訂版）在開曼                               exempted company with limited liability under the Company
     群島註冊成立為一家獲豁免有限公司，其                               Law (2002 Revision) of the Cayman Islands on 13 March 2003
     股份於二零零九年十月六日在香港聯合交                               and its shares were listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
     易所有限公司上市。本公司的直接控股公                               Limited on 6 October 2009. Its immediate holding company is
     司是在英屬處女群島註冊成立的華潤集團                               CRH (Cement) Limited, a company incorporated in the British
     （水泥）有限公司。本公司的最終控股公司                              Virgin Islands. Its ultimate holding company is China Resources
     是於中國內地成立的中國華潤有限公司。                               Company Limited, a company established in the Chinese
                                                      Mainland.


     本公司為一間投資控股公司，而其附屬                                The Company is an investment holding company and its
     公司（於本報告內與本公司統稱為「本集                               subsidiaries (together with the Company hereinafter collectively
     團」）主要從事生產及銷售水泥、混凝土及                              referred to as the “Group”) are principally engaged in the
     其他相關產品及服務。                                       manufacture and sale of cement, concrete and other related
                                                      products and services.


     綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，而本                             The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong Kong
     公司的功能貨幣為人民幣（「人民幣」） 。                             dollars (“HK$”) while the functional currency of the Company
     本公司選擇港元為其呈列貨幣，原因是管                               is Renminbi (“RMB”). The Company has adopted HK$ as its
     理層認為其更有利於綜合財務報表的使用                               presentation currency because the management considered
     者。                                               it is more beneficial to the users of the consolidated financial
                                                      statements.

     告準則」）（ 續）                     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (Continued)

     香港財務報告準則第15號來自客戶合             HKFRS 15 Revenue from contracts with customers
     約的收益


     香港財務報告準則第15號及其修訂本取代           HKFRS 15 and its amendments replace HKAS 11 “Construction
     香港會計準則第11號「建築合約」、香港會          contracts”, HKAS 18 “Revenue” and related interpretations and
     計準則第18號「收益」及相關詮釋，且（除          it applies, with limited exceptions, to all revenue arising from
     少數例外情況外）其應用於客戶合約產生            contracts with customers. HKFRS 15 establishes a new five-
     之所有收入。香港財務報告準則第15號就           step model to account for revenue arising from contracts with
     來自客戶合約之收入入賬確立一個新五             customers. Under HKFRS 15, revenue is recognized at an
     步模式。根據香港財務報告準則第15號，           amount that reflects the consideration to which an entity expects
     收入按能反映實體預期就向客戶轉讓貨物            to be entitled in exchange for transferring goods or services
     或服務而有權在交換中獲取之代價金額進            to a customer. The principles in HKFRS 15 provide a more
     行確認。香港財務報告準則第15號的原則           structured approach for measuring and recognizing revenue. The
     為就計量及確認收入提供更為結構化的方            standard also introduces extensive qualitative and quantitative
     法。該準則亦引入廣泛的定性及定量披露            disclosure requirements, including disaggregation of total revenue,
     規定，包括分拆收益總額、關於履約義務            information about performance obligations, changes in contract
     的資料、不同期間之間的合約資產及負債            asset and liability account balances between periods and key
     賬目結餘變動以及主要判斷及估計的資             judgements and estimates. The disclosures are included in
     料。該等披露資料載於財務報表附註5。由           Note 5 to the financial statements. As a result of the application
     於應用香港財務報告準則第15號，本集團           of HKFRS 15, the Group has changed the accounting policy
     已變更有關於財務報表附註3確認收益之            with respect to revenue recognition in Note 3 to the financial
     會計處理政策。                       statements.


     本集團採用經修訂追溯調整法採納香港財            The Group has adopted HKFRS 15 using the modified
     務報告準則第15號。根據此方法，該準則           retrospective method of adoption. Under this method, the
     既可應用於初始應用日期之所有合約，亦            standard can be applied either to all contracts at the date of initial
     可僅應用於該日尚未完成之合約。本集團            application or only to contracts that are not completed at this date.
     已選擇將該準則應用於二零一八年一月一            The Group has elected to apply the standard to contracts that are
     日尚未完成之合約。                     not completed as at 1 January 2018.

3.   重大會計政策                       3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES

     綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師公會頒佈                The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in
     的香港財務報告準則編製。此外，綜合財                accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition, the
     務報表包括香港聯合交易所有限公司證券                consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures
     上市規則及香港公司條例所規定的適用披                required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The
     露事項。                              Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the Hong Kong
                                       Companies Ordinance.

3.    重大會計政策（ 續）                                   3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES
                                                        (Continued)


      公平價值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序                                Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or
      交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付                                paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market
      的價格，而不論該價格是否可使用其他估                                participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that
      值方法直接可觀察或估計。於估計資產或                                price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation
      負債的公平價值時，本集團會考慮市場參                                technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the
      與者於計量日對資產或負債定價時所考慮                                Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or liability
      的資產或負債特點。該等綜合財務報表中                                if market participants would take those characteristics into account
      作計量及╱或披露用途的公平價值乃按此                                when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair
      基準釐定，惟香港財務報告準則第2號範                                value for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these
      圍內的以股份支付交易、香港會計準則第                                consolidated financial statements is determined on such a basis,
      17號範圍內的租賃交易，以及與公平價值                               except for share-based payment transactions that are within the
      相似但並非公平價值的項目計量（例如：                                scope of HKFRS 2, leasing transactions that are within the scope
      香港會計準則第2號的可變現淨值或香港                                of HKAS 17, and measurements that have some similarities to fair
      會計準則第36號的使用價值）除外。                                 value but are not fair value, such as net realizable value in HKAS 2
                                                        or value in use in HKAS 36.


      此外，就財務呈報而言，公平價值計量根                                In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
      據公平價值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及                                measurements are categorized into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
      公平價值計量的輸入數據對其整體的重要                                degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
      性分類為第一、第二或第三級，詳情如下：                               observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
                                                        measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:


      •      第一級輸入數據為實體有能力於                             •       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
             計量日獲得的就相同的資產或負                                     markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can
             債於活躍市場的報價（未調整）
                          ；                                     access at the measurement date;


      •      第二級輸入數據為不包括第一級                             •       Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices
             報價的資產或負債的可直接或間                                     included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset or
             接觀察的輸入數據；及                                         liability, either directly or indirectly; and


      •      第三級輸入數據為資產或負債的                             •       Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or
             不可觀察輸入數據。                                          liability.

     綜合基準                        Basis of consolidation


     綜合財務報表將本公司及其附屬公司與其          The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
     所控制實體的財務報表綜合入賬。當本公          statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
     司出現以下情況時則視為取得控制權：           Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                                 Company:


     •   可對被投資公司行使權力；            •       has power over the investee;


     •   就來自參與被投資公司的可變回          •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
         報中承受風險或享有權利；及                   involvement with the investee; and


     •   有能力行使權力以影響其回報。          •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.


     倘事實及情況顯示以上所列三種控制情況          The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts
     任何一種或以上出現變動，本集團會就其          and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more
     是否取得被投資公司的控制權作重新評           of the three elements of control listed above.
     估。


     於年內所收購或出售的一間附屬公司的收          Income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of
     入及費用自本集團取得控制權之日起計入          during the year are included in the consolidated statement of
     綜合全面收益表，直至本集團不再控制該          comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control until
     附屬公司之日為止。                   the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.


     損益及其他全面收益各部分乃分配至本公          Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive income
     司擁有人及非控股權益。附屬公司的全面          are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the non-
     收益總額乃分配至本公司擁有人及非控股          controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of subsidiaries is
     權益，即使此舉導致非控股權益出現虧絀          attributed to the owners of the Company and to the non-controlling
     結餘。                         interests even if this results in the non-controlling interests having
                                 a deficit balance.


     當必要時，本集團會對附屬公司的財務報          When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial
     表作出調整，使其會計政策與本集團其他          statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies in line
     成員公司所採用的保持一致。               with those used by other members of the Group.


     所有集團內部交易、結餘、收入及費用均          All intra-group transactions, balances, income and expenses are
     於綜合賬目悉數對銷。                  eliminated in full on consolidation.

     於產生時於損益中確認。                  for control of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally

      廠房及設備            3至40年                            Plant and machinery           3 to 40 years

      其他               3至35年                            Others                        3 to 35 years

      固定資產項目於出售或預期持續使用該資                                An item of fixed assets is derecognized upon disposal or when
      產不會產生未來經濟利益時取消確認。出                                no future economic benefits are expected to arise from the
      售或報廢一項固定資產產生的任何收益或                                continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss arising on the disposal
      虧損釐定為該資產的銷售所得款項與其賬                                or retirement of an item of fixed assets is determined as the
      面值的差額，並於損益內確認。                                    difference between the sales proceeds and the carrying amount of
                                                        the asset and is recognized in profit or loss.

     投資物業                        Investment properties

     接應佔支出。初步確認後，投資物業按其          directly attributable expenditure. Subsequent to initial recognition,

     內。                          are included in profit or loss for the period in which they arise.


     投資物業於出售，或當投資物業永久不再          An investment property is derecognized upon disposal or when
     使用，且預期自其出售中不會產生未來經          the investment property is permanently withdrawn from use and
     濟利益時取消確認。該物業取消確認所產          no future economic benefits are expected from its disposals. Any
     生的任何盈虧（按該資產出售所得款項淨          gain or loss arising on derecognition of the property (calculated as

      出一致同意的決定時存在。                                      parties sharing control.

      面收益而作出調整。於該聯營公司或合營                                thereafter to recognize the Group’s share of the profit or loss and

     平淨值超出投資成本的任何差額，於重新           the investment. Any excess of the Group’s share of the net fair
     評估後即時於投資被收購之期間於損益確           value of the identifiable assets and liabilities over the cost of the
     認。                           investment, after reassessment, is recognized immediately in profit
                                  or loss in the period in which the investment is acquired.

     聯營公司或合營公司於終止使用權益法當           accordance with HKFRS 9. The difference between the carrying
     日的賬面值與任何保留權益的公平價值及           amount of the associate or joint venture at the date the equity

     減值（ 不包括商譽）                  Impairment (other than goodwill)


     本集團於報告期末均會檢討其有形資產及          At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the carrying
     無形資產的賬面值，以釐定有否任何跡象          amounts of its tangible and intangible assets to determine
     顯示該等資產出現減值虧損。如有任何該          whether there is any indication that those assets have suffered
     等跡象，則會估計資產的可收回金額，以          an impairment loss. If any such indication exists, the recoverable
     釐定減值虧損（如有）的程度。              amount of the asset is estimated in order to determine the extent
                                 of the impairment loss, if any.


     倘不大可能估計個別資產的可收回金額，          When it is not possible to estimate the recoverable amount of an

     確定合理及一致的分配基準的最小組別現          units, or otherwise they are allocated to the smallest group of cash-
     金產生單位。                      generating units for which a reasonable and consistent allocation
                                 basis can be identified.


     可收回金額為公平價值減出售成本與使用          Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of
     價值之間的較高者。於評估使用價值時，          disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the estimated
     估計未來現金流量會採用反映現時市場對          future cash flows are discounted to their present value using a pre-
     貨幣時間價值及資產（或現金產生單位）          tax discount rate that reflects current market assessments of the
     特定風險評估的稅前貼現率，貼現至其現          time value of money and the risks specific to the asset (or a cash
     有價值，且并未就此對未來現金流量的估          generating unit) for which the estimates of future cash flows have

      金額低於其賬面值，則該項資產（或現金                                is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the carrying

      額。於分配減值虧損時，首先分配減值虧                                its recoverable amount. In allocating the impairment loss, the

      後按照單位內各項資產之賬面值比例分配                                any goodwill (if applicable) and then to the other assets on a pro-
      至其他資產。資產賬面值不會下調至低於                                rata basis based on the carrying amount of each asset in the unit.
      公平價值減去出售成本（若可計量）的所                                The carrying amount of an asset is not reduced below the highest
      得數額、其使用價值（若可釐定）及零之最                               of its fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable), its value in
      高者。本應分配至該資產的減值虧損金額                                use (if determinable) and zero. The amount of the impairment loss
      按比例分配至該單位的其他資產。減值虧                                that would otherwise have been allocated to the asset is allocated
      損會即時於損益確認。                                        pro rata to the other assets of the unit. An impairment loss is

      倘減值虧損於其後撥回，則該資產（或現                                When an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying

      現金產生單位）確認減值虧損而釐定的賬                                that would have been determined had no impairment loss been
      面值。所撥回的減值虧損即時於損益確                                 recognized for the asset (or a cash-generating unit) in prior years.
      認。                                                A reversal of an impairment loss is recognized immediately in profit

      稅項                                                Taxation

      總和。                                               payable and deferred tax.


      即期應付稅項乃按年內應課稅利潤計算。                                The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.
      應課稅利潤不包括其他年度的應課稅或應                                Taxable profit differs from “profit before taxation” as reported in
      扣減的收入或開支項目，亦不包括從未課                                the consolidated statement of comprehensive income because it
      稅或可扣減的項目，故與綜合全面收益表                                excludes items of income or expense that are taxable or deductible
      所列「除稅前盈利」不同。本集團的即期稅                               in other years and it further excludes items that are never taxable
      項乃按於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已頒佈                                or deductible. The Group’s current tax is calculated using tax rates
      的稅率計算。                                            that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the
                                                        reporting period.

      般就所有應課稅暫時差異而予以確認。遞                                computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally
      延稅項資產一般乃於有可能有應課稅利潤                                recognized for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax
      可動用可扣減暫時差異時予以確認。倘若                                assets are generally recognized for all deductible temporary
      暫時差異由商譽或一項不影響應課稅利潤                                differences to the extent that it is probable that taxable profits will
      或會計利潤的交易（業務合併除外）中初                                be available against which those deductible temporary differences
      次確認的資產及負債所產生，則不會確認                                can be utilized. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not
      有關遞延稅項資產及負債。此外，倘暫時                                recognized if the temporary difference arises from goodwill or from
      差額產生自商譽的內部確認，則遞延稅項                                the initial recognition (other than in a business combination) of
      負債不獲確認。                                           assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable
                                                        profit nor the accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities
                                                        are not recognized if the temporary difference arises from internal

      異而確認，惟若本集團可控制暫時差異撥                                and joint ventures, except where the Group is able to control the
      回，以及暫時差異可能不會於可見將來撥                                reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that the

     金融資產減值（ 自二零一八年一月一             Impairment of financial assets (policies under HKFRS 9
     日起適用的香港財務報告準則第9號項             applicable from 1 January 2018)
     下的政策）


     本集團就並非按公平價值計入損益持有的            The Group recognizes an allowance for expected credit losses
     所有債務工具確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信           (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
     貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據           profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
     合約到期的合約現金流量與本集團預期收            contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all
     取並按原始實際利率的概約利率折現的所            the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an
     有現金流量之間的差額釐定。預期現金流            approximation of the original effective interest rate. The expected
     量將包括出售所持抵押品或合約條款所包            cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of collateral held
     含的其他信貸升級措施所得的現金流量。            or other credit enhancements that are integral to the contractual
                                   terms.


     一般法                           General approach

     預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就首次確            ECLs are recognized in two stages. For credit exposures for which
     認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸敞口            there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial
     而言，會為未來12個月（12個月預期信貸          recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from
     虧損）可能發生的違約事件所產生的信貸            default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a 12
     虧損計提預期信貸虧損撥備。就首次確認            month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has been
     以來信貸風險大幅增加的信貸敞口而言，            a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss
     須就預期於敝口的餘下年期產生的信貸虧            allowance is required for credit losses expected over the remaining
     損計提虧損撥備，不論違約的時間（全期            life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
     預期信貸虧損）。                      lifetime ECL).

     按公平價值計入其他全面收入之債務投資           Debt investments at fair value through other comprehensive
     及按攤銷成本列賬之金融資產須根據一般           income and financial assets at amortized cost are subject to

                                  below.

     階段一 － 自首次確認起信貸風險並無           Stage 1 –     Financial instruments for which credit risk has not
           大幅增加且並按等同於12個                        increased significantly since initial recognition and for
           月預期信貸虧損之金額計量                         which the loss allowance is measured at an amount
           虧損撥備之金融工具                            equal to 12 month ECLs
     階段二 － 自首次確認起信貸風險已大           Stage 2 –     Financial instruments for which credit risk has
           幅增加而並非為信貸減值金                         increased significantly since initial recognition but
           融資產且按等同於全期預期                         that are not credit-impaired financial assets and for

     簡化法                          Simplified approach

     就不包含重大融資組成部份或當本集團應           For trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing
     用並無調整重大融資組成部份影響之實際           component or when the Group applies the practical expedient
     權益法之應收貿易賬款而言，本集團於計           of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component,
     算預期信貸虧損時應用簡化法。根據該簡           the Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs.
     化法，本集團並無追蹤信貸風險的變化，           Under the simplified approach, the Group does not track changes
     反而於各報告日期根據全期預期信貸虧損           in credit risk, but instead recognizes a loss allowance based on
     確認虧損撥備。本集團已設立根據本集團           lifetime ECLs at each reporting date. The Group has established
     過往信貸虧損經驗計算的撥備矩陣，並按           a provision matrix that is based on its historical credit loss
     債務人特定的前瞻性因素及經濟環境作出           experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors specific to the
     調整。                          debtors and the economic environment.

      政策）

      金融資產於各報告期末被評估是否有減值                                Financial assets are assessed for indicators of impairment at the

      被視為已減值。                                           the financial asset, the estimated future cash flows of the financial
                                                        assets have been affected.

      減值的客觀證據可包括︰                                       Objective evidence of impairment could include:

      •      發行人或對手方遇到嚴重財政困                             •       significant financial difficulty of the issuer or counterparty;
             難；或                                                or

             組。                                                 bankruptcy or financial re-organization.

      的客觀減值證據可包括本集團的過往收款                                collective basis. Objective evidence of impairment for a portfolio of

      次數增加，以及與應收款項逾期有關的全                                payments, an increase in the number of delayed payments in
      國或地方經濟狀況明顯改變。                                     the portfolio past the average credit period, as well as observable
                                                        changes in national or local economic conditions that correlate
                                                        with default on receivables.

      報告準則的會計政策編製，並定期由總裁                                accordance with accounting policies conformed with HKFRSs,
      進行審核以向經營分部配置資源及評估彼                                that are regularly reviewed by the chief executive officer in order to
      等的表現。                                             allocate resources to the operating segments and to assess their
                                                        performance.

12. 退休福利計劃                                         12. RETIREMENT BENEFIT SCHEMES

      香港僱員可參與本集團的界定供款退休計                               Employees in Hong Kong may be offered to participate in the
      劃或參加強制性公積金計劃（「強制性公                               Group’s defined contribution retirement schemes or to join the
      積金計劃」）
           。界定供款退休計劃的資產由                               Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (“MPF”). The assets of the
      獨立管理基金單獨持有。供款的金額乃以                               defined contribution retirement schemes are held separately in
      僱員基本薪金的特定百分比為基準，並於                               independently administered funds. The amount of contributions is
      綜合全面收益表中扣除。離職僱員無權享                               based on a specified percentage of the basic salaries of employees
      有的任何沒收供款則用以減低本集團的供                               and is charged to the consolidated statement of comprehensive
      款。於各報告期末，並無重大金額的未動                               income. Any forfeited contributions in respect of unvested benefits
      用沒收供款。強制性公積金計劃下的資產                               of staff leavers will be used to reduce the Group’s contributions.
      與本集團的資產在受託人的控制下由基金                               There was no significant amount of unutilized forfeited
      分別持有。本集團及各僱員每月向強制性                               contributions at the end of each reporting period. The assets
      公積金計劃供款。                                         under the MPF are held separately from those of the Group in
                                                       funds under the control of the trustee. The Group and each of the
                                                       employees make monthly contributions to the MPF.


      本集團在中國內地的僱員均屬於中國內地                               The employees of the Group in the Chinese Mainland are
      各自地方政府經營的政府管理退休福利計                               members of government-managed retirement benefit schemes
      劃的成員。本集團須向退休福利計劃支付                               operated by the respective local government in the Chinese
      工資特定百分比的供款作為福利資金。本                               Mainland. The Group is required to contribute a specified
      集團於該等計劃中的唯一責任為支付特定                               percentage of payroll cost to the retirement benefit schemes
      供款。本集團於中國內地的僱員亦獲提供                               to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with
      參與本集團定額供款退休金計劃。                                  respect to these schemes is to make the specified contributions.
                                                       Employees of the Group in the Chinese Mainland are also offered
                                                       to participate in the Group’s defined contribution retirement
                                                       schemes.

38. 資本風險管理                      38. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT

   本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團的實體                  The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group
   將可以持續經營，並透過優化債務及權益                  will be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the
   結餘為持份者帶來最大回報。於兩個年                   return to stakeholders through the optimization of the debt and
   度，本集團的整體策略維持不變。                     equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged
                                       for both years.

   本集團的資本架構包括債務（其中包括附                  The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which
   註35的銀行貸款、附註36的無抵押中期票                includes the bank loans in Note 35, unsecured medium term

   行股本及儲備） 。                           Company, comprising issued share capital and reserves.

   本公司管理層每半年審核一次資本架構。                  The management of the Company reviews the capital structure
   作為此審核的一部分，管理層會考慮資本                  on a semi-annual basis. As part of this review, the management
   成本及與各類別資本相關的風險。本集團                  considers the cost of capital and the risks associated with each

  本公司於二零零九年九月二日採納一項股            The Company adopted a share award scheme on 2 September
  份獎勵計劃。根據上市規則十七章，股份            2009. The share award scheme does not constitute a share
  獎勵計劃並不構成一項股份期權計劃且為            option scheme pursuant to Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules and
  本公司的一項酌情計劃。股份獎勵計劃旨            is a discretionary scheme of the Company. The purpose of the
  在確認本集團管理層及僱員（不包括任何            share award scheme is to recognize the contributions of officers
  董事）過去為本集團的發展作出的貢獻，            and employees of our Group, excluding any Director, towards the
  或為鼓勵經甄選承授人為本集團實現超越            development of our Group in the past or as incentives to selected
  目標的盈利及將經甄選人的利益與本集團            grantees to achieve higher than target profits for our Group and
  的持續增長及發展掛鈎。股份獎勵計劃的            to align the interests of the selected grantees with sustainable
  有效期為十年，直至二零一九年九月二日            growth and development of our Group. The share award scheme
  屆滿，除非董事局於較早日期酌情終止。            is effective for a term of ten years until 2 September 2019 unless
                                terminated at the discretion of the Board at an earlier date.
